output.md
Task 6 is implemented and verified, but not committed: this environment has no git identity configured, so the four files are staged and the commit is left for you.

**Changed**
- `CardioLogField.kt` (new; renamed from `CardioLogFields.kt` because ktlint requires a file holding a single class to take its name). Holds the `CardioLogField` enum and `logFieldsOf(activity)`, the per-activity field table in sheet order.
- `CardioActivity.kt`: adds `val logFields` delegating to that table.
- `CardioLogDraft.kt` (new): the immutable draft data class with `text`, `with`, `left`, `shownError`, `errors`, `canSubmit`, `derived`, `toRequest`, and the companion `fresh` and `of`. Derived pace and speed come from `CardioFormat.derivedPace` and `derivedSpeed`.
- `CardioLogDraftTest.kt` (new): the fourteen named tests.

**Runs**
- RED: `./gradlew --no-configuration-cache :shared:desktopTest --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` failed to compile (unresolved `CardioLogDraft`, `CardioLogField`, `logFields`).
- GREEN: same command after the implementation; XML shows `tests="14" failures="0" errors="0"`.
- `:shared:ktlintFormat` ran clean after the file rename. `:shared:ktlintCheck :shared:detekt :shared:desktopTest --rerun --tests '*CardioLogDraftTest'` exited 0. detekt's ReturnCount on `parseClock` was fixed and rerun.
- I did not run the module suite or an old-vs-new comparison, since this task refactors nothing.

**Decisions and gaps to check**
- The backend's `CardioActivity.logFields` and `CardioLogSpecification` are not in this checkout, so the table and ranges are from the task text, not checked against the backend.
- L4's frame figures are not in the repo. The rower test uses 8:24 over 2000 m (derives `2:06`).
- Choices the task did not specify:
  - Duration out of range shows `between 0:01 and 24:00:00`.
  - An unparseable pace shows the pace range message, not a new error text.
  - Unparseable numbers show the range message.
  - Distance bounds are 0.01 to 1000 km (1,000,000 m for a rower), inclusive.
  - Calories use `kcal`.
  - `of` derives the saved distance into the workout's unit (rower stays in metres).
- `toRequest()` uses `checkNotNull` for duration, relying on the caller gating on `canSubmit`.

**Needs you**
- Set a git identity, then commit the staged files with the message from the plan and the `Co-Authored-By: Claude Code <noreply@anthropic.com>` trailer. I did not change git config or invent an author.

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.math.floor
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val HOUR = 3600
+private const val MAX_DURATION = 86_400.0
+private const val MAX_ELEVATION = 20_000.0
+private const val MAX_POWER = 2_500.0
+private const val MAX_SPEED = 120.0
+private const val MAX_PACE = 3_600.0
+private const val ROWER_MAX_PACE = 600.0
+private const val MAX_METRES = 1_000_000.0
+private const val MIN_KM = 0.01
+private const val HUNDREDTHS = 100.0
+
+/**
+ * A log sheet's values as typed. [texts] holds every field typed as text; [effort] and [note] are
+ * properties of their own; [left] is the fields the user has left, which alone show their errors.
+ * A rower's distance is metres whatever [unit] the workout is in.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val unit: DistanceUnit,
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.toIntOrNull())
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> = CardioLogField.entries.mapNotNull { field -> problem(field)?.let { field to it } }.toMap()
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = duration() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    /** The value a blank pace or speed is worked out to from duration and distance; null once typed. */
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedPace(duration(), distance(), null, activity, unit)?.let { clock(it) }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(duration(), distance(), null, unit)?.toString()
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = checkNotNull(duration()),
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = parseClock(text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE), allowHours = false),
+            avgSpeed = decimalOf(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED),
+            avgHeartRate = wholeOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = wholeOf(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = wholeOf(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = wholeOf(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = wholeOf(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = wholeOf(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    private fun duration(): Int? = parseClock(text(CardioLogField.DURATION), allowHours = true)
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? {
+        val value = decimalOf(CardioLogField.DISTANCE) ?: return null
+        return CardioDistance(value, if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else unit)
+    }
+
+    private fun wholeOf(field: CardioLogField): Int? = text(field).toIntOrNull()
+
+    private fun decimalOf(field: CardioLogField): Double? = text(field).toDoubleOrNull()
+
+    private fun valueOf(field: CardioLogField): Double? =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DURATION -> duration()?.toDouble()
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> parseClock(text(field), allowHours = false)?.toDouble()
+            CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> decimalOf(field)
+            else -> wholeOf(field)?.toDouble()
+        }
+
+    private fun problem(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (text(field).isBlank()) return null
+        if (field == CardioLogField.DURATION && duration() == null) return "use h:mm:ss"
+        val range = range(field) ?: return null
+        val value = valueOf(field)
+        return when {
+            value == null || value !in range.min..range.max -> range.message
+
+            field == CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE &&
+                wholeOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)?.let { value < it } == true -> "max heart rate is below average"
+
+            else -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    private fun range(field: CardioLogField): Range? {
+        val rower = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DURATION -> {
+                Range(1.0, MAX_DURATION, "") { durationText(it.toInt()) }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> {
+                if (rower) {
+                    Range(1.0, MAX_METRES, "m")
+                } else {
+                    Range(MIN_KM, MAX_METRES / unit.metres, unit.name.lowercase())
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                Range(60.0, if (rower) ROWER_MAX_PACE else MAX_PACE, CardioMetric.PACE.unitLabel(activity, unit).orEmpty()) {
+                    clock(it.toInt())
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                Range(0.1, MAX_SPEED, CardioMetric.SPEED.unitLabel(activity, unit).orEmpty())
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+                Range(0.0, MAX_ELEVATION, if (unit == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+                Range(30.0, 250.0, "bpm")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> {
+                if (rower) {
+                    Range(10.0, 60.0, "spm")
+                } else {
+                    Range(1.0, 250.0, CardioMetric.CADENCE.unitLabel(activity, unit).orEmpty())
+                }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> {
+                Range(1.0, MAX_POWER, "W")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.CALORIES -> {
+                Range(0.0, 20_000.0, "kcal")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    companion object {
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val started = workout.startedAt
+            val elapsed =
+                if (workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS && started != null) {
+                    (now - started).inWholeSeconds.toInt()
+                } else {
+                    null
+                }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                unit = workout.distanceUnit,
+                texts = elapsed?.let { mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText(it)) }.orEmpty(),
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val base = CardioLogDraft(activity = workout.activity, unit = workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val log = workout.log ?: return base
+            val texts =
+                listOfNotNull(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText(log.durationSeconds),
+                    log.distance?.let { CardioLogField.DISTANCE to plain(distanceIn(it, workout)) },
+                    log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_PACE to clock(it) },
+                    log.avgSpeed?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED to plain(it) },
+                    log.elevationGain?.let { CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN to it.toString() },
+                    log.avgHeartRate?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to it.toString() },
+                    log.maxHeartRate?.let { CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to it.toString() },
+                    log.avgCadence?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to it.toString() },
+                    log.avgPower?.let { CardioLogField.AVG_POWER to it.toString() },
+                    log.calories?.let { CardioLogField.CALORIES to it.toString() },
+                ).toMap()
+            return base.copy(texts = texts, effort = log.effort)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private class Range(
+    val min: Double,
+    val max: Double,
+    val unit: String,
+    val show: (Double) -> String = ::plain,
+) {
+    val message: String
+        get() = "between ${show(min)} and ${show(max)}" + if (unit.isEmpty()) "" else " $unit"
+}
+
+private fun distanceIn(
+    distance: CardioDistance,
+    workout: CardioWorkout,
+): Double {
+    val metres = distance.value * distance.unit.metres
+    return if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) metres else metres / workout.distanceUnit.metres
+}
+
+/** `m:ss`, or `h:mm:ss` when [allowHours]; seconds and minutes after a colon are two digits under 60. */
+private fun parseClock(
+    text: String,
+    allowHours: Boolean,
+): Int? {
+    val parts = text.split(":")
+    val hasHours = allowHours && parts.size == 3
+    if (parts.size != 2 && !hasHours) return null
+    val seconds = sexagesimal(parts.last()) ?: return null
+    val minutes = (if (hasHours) sexagesimal(parts[1]) else digits(parts[0])) ?: return null
+    val hours = if (hasHours) digits(parts[0]) ?: return null else 0
+    return hours * HOUR + minutes * 60 + seconds
+}
+
+private fun digits(part: String): Int? = part.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() && it.all(Char::isDigit) }?.toIntOrNull()
+
+private fun sexagesimal(part: String): Int? = part.takeIf { it.length == 2 && it.all(Char::isDigit) }?.toInt()?.takeIf { it < 60 }
+
+private fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < HOUR) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+private fun plain(value: Double): String {
+    val rounded = (value * HUNDREDTHS).roundToLong() / HUNDREDTHS
+    return if (rounded == floor(rounded)) rounded.toLong().toString() else rounded.toString()
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val runFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+private val rideFields =
+    listOf(
+        CardioLogField.DURATION,
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+        CardioLogField.NOTE,
+    )
+
+/** The backend's `CardioActivity.logFields` sets, each in the sheet's order (L1, L3, L4 and step-4's prose). */
+internal fun logFieldsOf(activity: CardioActivity): List<CardioLogField> =
+    when (activity) {
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+            runFields
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+            runFields - setOf(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            rideFields
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+            rideFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            )
+        }
+
+        CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            )
+        }
+    }

+        assertEquals(1992, short.toRequest().durationSeconds)

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.number
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+private const val DAY_SECONDS = 86_400
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's state: each field's typed text, the effort and note, and the fields the user has
+ * left (a range error shows only once its field is left). Pure — the sheet owns the debounce.
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    private val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    private val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String = texts[field].orEmpty()
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft = copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> =
+        buildMap {
+            for (field in activity.logFields) {
+                val typed = text(field)
+                if (typed.isBlank()) continue
+                problem(field, typed)?.let { put(field, it) }
+            }
+            if (CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE !in this) {
+                val avg = intOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE)
+                val max = intOf(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+                if (avg != null && max != null && max < avg) put(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "max heart rate is below average")
+            }
+        }
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = durationSeconds() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedPace(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, activity, units)?.let { clock(it) }
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(durationSeconds(), distance(), null, units)?.toString()
+            }
+
+            else -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = durationSeconds() ?: 0,
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = if (text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE).isBlank()) null else parseClock(text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)),
+            avgSpeed = numberOf(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED),
+            avgHeartRate = intOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = intOf(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = intOf(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = intOf(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = intOf(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = intOf(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    private fun durationSeconds(): Int? = parseDuration(text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+
+    private fun isRower() = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? {
+        val value = numberOf(CardioLogField.DISTANCE)?.takeIf { it > 0 } ?: return null
+        return CardioDistance(value, if (isRower()) DistanceUnit.M else units)
+    }
+
+    private fun intOf(field: CardioLogField): Int? = text(field).trim().toIntOrNull()
+
+    private fun numberOf(field: CardioLogField): Double? = text(field).trim().toDoubleOrNull()
+
+    private fun problem(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        typed: String,
+    ): String? {
+        if (field == CardioLogField.DURATION) {
+            val seconds = parseDuration(typed) ?: return "use h:mm:ss"
+            return if (seconds < 1 || seconds > DAY_SECONDS) "between 0:01 and 24:00:00" else null
+        }
+        val bounds = bounds(field) ?: return null
+        val value =
+            (if (field == CardioLogField.AVG_PACE) parseClock(typed)?.toDouble() else typed.trim().toDoubleOrNull())
+                ?: return bounds.message()
+        val inRange = if (field == CardioLogField.DISTANCE) value > bounds.min && value <= bounds.max else value in bounds.min..bounds.max
+        return if (inRange) null else bounds.message()
+    }
+
+    private fun bounds(field: CardioLogField): Bounds? =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> {
+                if (isRower()) Bounds(0.0, 1_000_000.0, "m") else Bounds(0.0, 1000.0, units.name.lowercase())
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+                if (isRower()) Bounds(60.0, 600.0, "/500 m", ::clockOf) else Bounds(60.0, 3600.0, "/${units.name.lowercase()}", ::clockOf)
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+                Bounds(0.1, 120.0, CardioMetric.SPEED.unitLabel(activity, units).orEmpty())
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+                Bounds(0.0, 20_000.0, if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+                Bounds(30.0, 250.0, "bpm")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> {
+                if (isRower()) Bounds(10.0, 60.0, "spm") else Bounds(1.0, 250.0, CardioMetric.CADENCE.unitLabel(activity, units).orEmpty())
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> {
+                Bounds(1.0, 2500.0, "W")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.CALORIES -> {
+                Bounds(0.0, 20_000.0, "kcal")
+            }
+
+            CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+                null
+            }
+        }
+
+    companion object {
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val base = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit)
+            val started = workout.startedAt
+            if (workout.status != WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS || started == null) return base
+            return base.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, durationText((now - started).inWholeSeconds.toInt()))
+        }
+
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val base = CardioLogDraft(workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit, note = workout.notes.orEmpty())
+            val log = workout.log ?: return base
+            val metric = if (workout.activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else workout.distanceUnit
+            val texts =
+                buildMap {
+                    put(CardioLogField.DURATION, durationText(log.durationSeconds))
+                    log.distance?.let { put(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, number(it.value * it.unit.metres / metric.metres)) }
+                    log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, clock(it)) }
+                    log.avgSpeed?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, number(it)) }
+                    log.elevationGain?.let { put(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgHeartRate?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.maxHeartRate?.let { put(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgCadence?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, it.toString()) }
+                    log.avgPower?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER, it.toString()) }
+                    log.calories?.let { put(CardioLogField.CALORIES, it.toString()) }
+                }
+            return base.copy(texts = texts, effort = log.effort)
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+private class Bounds(
+    val min: Double,
+    val max: Double,
+    val unit: String,
+    val format: (Double) -> String = { number(it) },
+) {
+    fun message() = "between ${format(min)} and ${format(max)} $unit"
+}
+
+private fun clockOf(seconds: Double) = clock(seconds.toInt())
+
+private fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < 3600) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+private val DURATION_TEXT = Regex("""(\d{1,4}):([0-5]\d)(?::([0-5]\d))?""")
+private val CLOCK_TEXT = Regex("""(\d{1,3}):([0-5]\d)""")
+
+private fun parseDuration(text: String): Int? {
+    val (a, b, c) = DURATION_TEXT.matchEntire(text.trim())?.destructured ?: return null
+    return if (c.isEmpty()) a.toInt() * 60 + b.toInt() else a.toInt() * 3600 + b.toInt() * 60 + c.toInt()
+}
+
+private fun parseClock(text: String): Int? {
+    val (m, s) = CLOCK_TEXT.matchEntire(text.trim())?.destructured ?: return null
+    return m.toInt() * 60 + s.toInt()
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}
+
+private val tail = listOf(CardioLogField.CALORIES, CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE)
+private val heart = listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)
+
+internal val CardioActivity.logFields: List<CardioLogField>
+    get() =
+        when (this) {
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN -> {
+                listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN) + heart +
+                    tail
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                ) +
+                    tail
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN) +
+                    heart + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) + tail
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE -> {
+                listOf(CardioLogField.DURATION, CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED) +
+                    heart + listOf(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER) + tail
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                ) +
+                    tail
+            }
+
+            CardioActivity.ROWER -> {
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                    CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                ) + tail
+            }
+        }

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalTime
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertNotNull
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val day = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16)
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = DistanceUnit.KM,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = day,
+        scheduledTime = LocalTime(18, 30),
+        displayName = "Easy run",
+        notes = notes,
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = CardioKind.EASY,
+        distanceUnit = unit,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.ELLIPTICAL.logFields,
+        )
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+        assertEquals(1992, draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(4360, draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40").toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals("33:12", draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12").text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+        for (typed in listOf("45", "1:75", "abc")) {
+            val bad = draft.with(CardioLogField.DURATION, typed).left(CardioLogField.DURATION)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", bad.errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION], typed)
+            assertEquals(false, bad.canSubmit, typed)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z")).with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "")
+        assertEquals(false, draft.canSubmit)
+        assertNull(draft.errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, "6:40")
+        assertNull(draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(400, draft.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(activity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val rower = workout(activity = CardioActivity.ROWER)
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(rower, Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "29:54")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "7120")
+        assertEquals("2:06", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(7120.0, DistanceUnit.M), draft.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "139")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "138")
+                .left(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE)
+        assertEquals("max heart rate is below average", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE])
+        assertEquals(1, draft.errors().size)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(activity = CardioActivity.ROWER), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+                .left(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE)
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", draft.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val typed =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "45")
+        assertNotNull(typed.errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION])
+        assertNull(typed.shownError(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", typed.left(CardioLogField.DURATION).shownError(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00Z")
+        val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = started), now)
+        assertEquals("41:06", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val saved =
+            CardioLog(
+                durationSeconds = 2700,
+                distance = CardioDistance(9.2, DistanceUnit.KM),
+                avgPaceSeconds = null,
+                derivedAvgPaceSeconds = 294,
+                avgHeartRate = 150,
+                maxHeartRate = 172,
+                calories = 620,
+                effort = 6,
+            )
+        val draft = CardioLogDraft.of(workout(log = saved, notes = "hilly"))
+        assertEquals("45:00", draft.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("9.2", draft.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("4:53", draft.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("150", draft.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals(6, draft.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", draft.note)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val draft =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(), Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:30:00Z"))
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "150")
+                .copy(effort = 6, note = "hilly")
+        val request = draft.toRequest()
+        assertEquals(1992, request.durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5.02, DistanceUnit.KM), request.distance)
+        assertEquals(150, request.avgHeartRate)
+        assertEquals(6, request.effort)
+        assertNull(request.avgPaceSeconds)
+        assertNull(request.avgSpeed)
+        assertTrue(request.toString().contains("effort=6"))
+        assertTrue(!request.toString().contains("hilly"))
+    }
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.hourClock
+import kotlin.math.floor
+import kotlin.math.roundToLong
+
+private val MINUTES_SECONDS = Regex("""(\d{1,4}):([0-5]\d)""")
+private val HOURS_MINUTES_SECONDS = Regex("""(\d{1,2}):([0-5]\d):([0-5]\d)""")
+
+internal class LogLimit(
+    val min: Double,
+    val max: Double,
+    val minText: String,
+    val maxText: String,
+    val unit: String?,
+) {
+    fun accepts(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: Double,
+    ): Boolean = if (field == CardioLogField.DISTANCE) value > min && value < max else value in min..max
+
+    fun message(): String = "between $minText and $maxText" + (unit?.let { " $it" } ?: "")
+}
+
+internal fun limitOf(
+    field: CardioLogField,
+    activity: CardioActivity,
+    units: DistanceUnit,
+): LogLimit? {
+    val rower = activity == CardioActivity.ROWER
+    return when (field) {
+        CardioLogField.DURATION -> {
+            LogLimit(1.0, 86_400.0, clock(1), hourClock(86_400), null)
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.DISTANCE -> {
+            if (rower) {
+                LogLimit(0.0, 1_000_000.0, "0", "1000000", "m")
+            } else {
+                LogLimit(0.0, 1000.0, "0", "1000", units.name.lowercase())
+            }
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> {
+            LogLimit(
+                60.0,
+                if (rower) 600.0 else 3600.0,
+                clock(60),
+                clock(if (rower) 600 else 3600),
+                CardioMetric.PACE.unitLabel(activity, units),
+            )
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> {
+            LogLimit(0.1, 120.0, "0.1", "120.0", CardioMetric.SPEED.unitLabel(activity, units))
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN -> {
+            LogLimit(0.0, 20_000.0, "0", "20000", if (units == DistanceUnit.MI) "ft" else "m")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE -> {
+            LogLimit(30.0, 250.0, "30", "250", "bpm")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE -> {
+            if (rower) {
+                LogLimit(10.0, 60.0, "10", "60", CardioMetric.CADENCE.unitLabel(activity, units))
+            } else {
+                LogLimit(1.0, 250.0, "1", "250", CardioMetric.CADENCE.unitLabel(activity, units))
+            }
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.AVG_POWER -> {
+            LogLimit(1.0, 2500.0, "1", "2500", "W")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.CALORIES -> {
+            LogLimit(0.0, 20_000.0, "0", "20000", "kcal")
+        }
+
+        CardioLogField.EFFORT, CardioLogField.NOTE -> {
+            null
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+internal fun durationSeconds(text: String): Int? {
+    val trimmed = text.trim()
+    val hms = HOURS_MINUTES_SECONDS.matchEntire(trimmed) ?: return paceSeconds(trimmed)
+    return hms.part(1) * 3600 + hms.part(2) * 60 + hms.part(3)
+}
+
+internal fun paceSeconds(text: String): Int? = MINUTES_SECONDS.matchEntire(text.trim())?.let { it.part(1) * 60 + it.part(2) }
+
+internal fun durationText(seconds: Int): String = if (seconds < 3600) clock(seconds) else hourClock(seconds)
+
+internal fun numberText(value: Double): String = if (value == floor(value)) value.toLong().toString() else value.toString()
+
+internal fun tenthsText(value: Double): String {
+    val tenths = (value * 10).roundToLong()
+    return "${tenths / 10}.${tenths % 10}"
+}
+
+private fun MatchResult.part(index: Int): Int = groupValues[index].toInt()

@@ -0,0 +1,169 @@
+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.cardio.shorthand.clock
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+/**
+ * The log sheet's values as typed: text per field, with effort and note held as values. Pure: a
+ * change returns a new draft, and nothing here is sent except through [toRequest].
+ */
+data class CardioLogDraft(
+    val activity: CardioActivity,
+    val units: DistanceUnit,
+    val effort: Int? = null,
+    val note: String = "",
+    val texts: Map<CardioLogField, String> = emptyMap(),
+    val left: Set<CardioLogField> = emptySet(),
+) {
+    private val distanceUnit: DistanceUnit get() = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+
+    fun text(field: CardioLogField): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> effort?.toString().orEmpty()
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> note
+            else -> texts[field].orEmpty()
+        }
+
+    fun with(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        value: String,
+    ): CardioLogDraft =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.EFFORT -> copy(effort = value.trim().toIntOrNull())
+            CardioLogField.NOTE -> copy(note = value)
+            else -> copy(texts = texts + (field to value))
+        }
+
+    fun left(field: CardioLogField): CardioLogDraft = copy(left = left + field)
+
+    fun shownError(field: CardioLogField): String? = if (field in left) errors()[field] else null
+
+    fun errors(): Map<CardioLogField, String> = CardioLogField.entries.mapNotNull { field -> problem(field)?.let { field to it } }.toMap()
+
+    val canSubmit: Boolean get() = duration() != null && errors().isEmpty()
+
+    fun derived(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        if (text(field).isNotBlank()) return null
+        return when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> CardioFormat.derivedPace(duration(), distance(), null, activity, units)?.let { clock(it) }
+            CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> CardioFormat.derivedSpeed(duration(), distance(), null, units)?.let { tenthsText(it) }
+            else -> null
+        }
+    }
+
+    fun toRequest(): CardioLogRequest =
+        CardioLogRequest(
+            durationSeconds = checkNotNull(duration()) { "toRequest needs a duration" },
+            distance = distance(),
+            avgPaceSeconds = paceSeconds(text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)),
+            avgSpeed = text(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED).trim().toDoubleOrNull(),
+            avgHeartRate = whole(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE),
+            maxHeartRate = whole(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE),
+            calories = whole(CardioLogField.CALORIES),
+            effort = effort,
+            elevationGain = whole(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN),
+            avgCadence = whole(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE),
+            avgPower = whole(CardioLogField.AVG_POWER),
+        )
+
+    private fun duration(): Int? = durationSeconds(text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+
+    private fun distance(): CardioDistance? =
+        text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE).trim().toDoubleOrNull()?.let { CardioDistance(it, distanceUnit) }
+
+    private fun whole(field: CardioLogField): Int? = text(field).trim().toIntOrNull()
+
+    private fun problem(field: CardioLogField): String? {
+        val typed = text(field).trim()
+        val limit = limitOf(field, activity, units) ?: return null
+        if (typed.isEmpty()) return null
+        val value = number(field, typed) ?: return shapeProblem(field, limit)
+        if (!limit.accepts(field, value)) return limit.message()
+        return if (field == CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE && belowAverage(value)) "max heart rate is below average" else null
+    }
+
+    private fun belowAverage(max: Double): Boolean {
+        val average = whole(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE) ?: return false
+        return max < average
+    }
+
+    private fun number(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        typed: String,
+    ): Double? =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DURATION -> durationSeconds(typed)?.toDouble()
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> paceSeconds(typed)?.toDouble()
+            CardioLogField.DISTANCE, CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED -> typed.toDoubleOrNull()
+            else -> typed.toIntOrNull()?.toDouble()
+        }
+
+    private fun shapeProblem(
+        field: CardioLogField,
+        limit: LogLimit,
+    ): String =
+        when (field) {
+            CardioLogField.DURATION -> "use h:mm:ss"
+            CardioLogField.AVG_PACE -> "use m:ss"
+            else -> limit.message()
+        }
+
+    companion object {
+        fun fresh(
+            workout: CardioWorkout,
+            now: Instant,
+        ): CardioLogDraft {
+            val elapsed =
+                workout.startedAt
+                    ?.takeIf { workout.status == WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS }
+                    ?.let { (now - it).inWholeSeconds.coerceAtLeast(0) }
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+                texts = elapsed?.let { mapOf(CardioLogField.DURATION to durationText(it.toInt())) }.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        fun of(workout: CardioWorkout): CardioLogDraft {
+            val log = workout.log
+            return CardioLogDraft(
+                activity = workout.activity,
+                units = workout.distanceUnit,
+                effort = log?.effort,
+                note = workout.notes.orEmpty(),
+                texts = log?.let { savedTexts(it, workout.activity, workout.distanceUnit) }.orEmpty(),
+            )
+        }
+
+        private fun savedTexts(
+            log: CardioLog,
+            activity: CardioActivity,
+            units: DistanceUnit,
+        ): Map<CardioLogField, String> =
+            buildMap {
+                put(CardioLogField.DURATION, durationText(log.durationSeconds))
+                log.distance?.let { put(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, numberText(distanceIn(it, activity, units))) }
+                log.avgPaceSeconds?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, clock(it)) }
+                log.avgSpeed?.let { put(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED, numberText(it)) }
+                listOf(
+                    CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN to log.elevationGain,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE to log.avgHeartRate,
+                    CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to log.maxHeartRate,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE to log.avgCadence,
+                    CardioLogField.AVG_POWER to log.avgPower,
+                    CardioLogField.CALORIES to log.calories,
+                ).forEach { (field, value) -> value?.let { put(field, it.toString()) } }
+            }
+
+        private fun distanceIn(
+            distance: CardioDistance,
+            activity: CardioActivity,
+            units: DistanceUnit,
+        ): Double {
+            val target = if (activity == CardioActivity.ROWER) DistanceUnit.M else units
+            return if (distance.unit == target) distance.value else distance.value * distance.unit.metres / target.metres
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+/** A row of the log sheet, in the order a sheet draws it per activity ([CardioActivity.logFields]). */
+enum class CardioLogField {
+    DURATION,
+    DISTANCE,
+    AVG_PACE,
+    AVG_SPEED,
+    ELEVATION_GAIN,
+    AVG_HEART_RATE,
+    MAX_HEART_RATE,
+    AVG_CADENCE,
+    AVG_POWER,
+    CALORIES,
+    EFFORT,
+    NOTE,
+}

+package com.gymie.shared.cardio
+
+import com.gymie.shared.domain.WorkoutStatus
+import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
+import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
+import kotlin.test.Test
+import kotlin.test.assertEquals
+import kotlin.test.assertFailsWith
+import kotlin.test.assertFalse
+import kotlin.test.assertNull
+import kotlin.test.assertTrue
+import kotlin.time.Instant
+
+class CardioLogDraftTest {
+    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }
+    private val km = DistanceUnit.KM
+    private val now = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T19:12:06.900Z")
+
+    private fun workout(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = km,
+        status: WorkoutStatus = WorkoutStatus.PLANNED,
+        startedAt: Instant? = null,
+        notes: String? = null,
+        log: CardioLog? = null,
+    ) = CardioWorkout(
+        id = "w1",
+        scheduledDate = LocalDate(2026, 10, 16),
+        displayName = "Intervals",
+        status = status,
+        startedAt = startedAt,
+        activity = activity,
+        kind = CardioKind.INTERVALS,
+        distanceUnit = unit,
+        notes = notes,
+        log = log,
+    )
+
+    private fun blank(
+        activity: CardioActivity = CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN,
+        unit: DistanceUnit = km,
+    ) = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(activity, unit), now)
+
+    @Test
+    fun eachActivityLogsItsOwnFieldsInFrameOrder() {
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.TREADMILL_RUN.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED,
+                CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE.logFields - CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN,
+            CardioActivity.INDOOR_RIDE.logFields,
+        )
+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,

+        assertEquals(
+            listOf(
+                CardioLogField.DURATION,
+                CardioLogField.DISTANCE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_PACE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_POWER,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE,
+                CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE,
+                CardioLogField.CALORIES,
+                CardioLogField.EFFORT,
+                CardioLogField.NOTE,
+            ),
+            CardioActivity.ROWER.logFields,
+        )

+
+    @Test
+    fun durationReadsHoursMinutesAndSeconds() {
+        val short = blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+        assertEquals(1992, short.toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        val long = blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40")
+        assertEquals(4360, long.toRequest().durationSeconds)
+        assertEquals("41:06", blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "41:06").text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("45", "1:75", "abc").forEach { text ->
+            val d = blank().with(CardioLogField.DURATION, text).left(CardioLogField.DURATION)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.errors()[CardioLogField.DURATION], text)
+            assertEquals("use h:mm:ss", d.shownError(CardioLogField.DURATION), text)
+            assertFalse(d.canSubmit, text)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceRefusesOtherShapes() {
+        listOf("6.37", "6:75", "397").forEach { text ->
+            val d = blank().with(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, text).left(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE)
+            assertEquals("use m:ss", d.shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE), text)
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun toRequestRefusesWithoutADuration() {
+        assertFailsWith<IllegalStateException> { blank().toRequest() }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anEmptyDurationHoldsThePrimary() {
+        val d = blank().left(CardioLogField.DURATION)
+        assertNull(d.shownError(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertFalse(d.errors().containsKey(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertFalse(d.canSubmit)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun paceIsDerivedWhenBlankAndNeverSent() {
+        val d =
+            blank()
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+        assertEquals("6:37", d.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aTypedPaceReplacesTheDerivedOne() {
+        val d =
+            blank()
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE, "6:30")
+        assertNull(d.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(390, d.toRequest().avgPaceSeconds)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun speedIsDerivedForRides() {
+        val d =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(CardioActivity.OUTDOOR_RIDE), now)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "1:12:40")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "38.6")
+        assertEquals("31.9", d.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_SPEED))
+        assertNull(d.toRequest().avgSpeed)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRowerDerivesPerFiveHundredMetres() {
+        val d =
+            CardioLogDraft
+                .fresh(workout(CardioActivity.ROWER), now)
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "25:00")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5950")
+        assertEquals("2:06", d.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals(CardioDistance(5950.0, DistanceUnit.M), d.toRequest().distance)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun maxBelowAvgIsAnError() {
+        val d =
+            blank()
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "139")
+        assertEquals(mapOf(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE to "max heart rate is below average"), d.errors())
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun rowerCadenceOutOfRangeNamesTheRange() {
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(CardioActivity.ROWER), now).with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.errors()[CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE])
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anErrorShowsOnlyOnceTheFieldWasLeft() {
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.fresh(workout(CardioActivity.ROWER), now).with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "8")
+        assertTrue(d.errors().containsKey(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertNull(d.shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("between 10 and 60 spm", d.left(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE).shownError(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun durationPrefillsFromTheClockRoundedDown() {
+        val started = Instant.parse("2026-10-16T18:31:00Z")
+        val d =
+            CardioLogDraft.fresh(
+                workout(status = WorkoutStatus.IN_PROGRESS, startedAt = started),
+                now,
+            )
+        assertEquals("41:06", d.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aSavedLogFillsTheDraft() {
+        val payload =
+            """
+            {
+              "id": "w2", "scheduledDate": "2026-10-14", "displayName": "Intervals",
+              "status": "FINISHED", "activity": "OUTDOOR_RUN", "kind": "INTERVALS",
+              "distanceUnit": "KM", "notes": "hilly",
+              "log": {
+                "durationSeconds": 2466, "distance": {"value": 5.02, "unit": "KM"},
+                "avgHeartRate": 148, "maxHeartRate": 171, "calories": 410, "effort": 6,
+                "elevationGain": 42, "avgCadence": 172, "derivedAvgPaceSeconds": 491
+              }
+            }
+            """.trimIndent()
+        val saved = json.decodeFromString<CardioWorkout>(payload)
+        val d = CardioLogDraft.of(saved)
+        assertEquals("41:06", d.text(CardioLogField.DURATION))
+        assertEquals("5.02", d.text(CardioLogField.DISTANCE))
+        assertEquals("", d.text(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("8:11", d.derived(CardioLogField.AVG_PACE))
+        assertEquals("148", d.text(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("171", d.text(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE))
+        assertEquals("172", d.text(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE))
+        assertEquals("42", d.text(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN))
+        assertEquals("410", d.text(CardioLogField.CALORIES))
+        assertEquals(6, d.effort)
+        assertEquals("hilly", d.note)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theRequestCarriesTypedFieldsOnly() {
+        val d =
+            blank()
+                .with(CardioLogField.DURATION, "33:12")
+                .with(CardioLogField.DISTANCE, "5.02")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_HEART_RATE, "148")
+                .with(CardioLogField.MAX_HEART_RATE, "171")
+                .with(CardioLogField.ELEVATION_GAIN, "42")
+                .with(CardioLogField.AVG_CADENCE, "172")
+                .with(CardioLogField.CALORIES, "410")
+                .with(CardioLogField.EFFORT, "6")
+                .with(CardioLogField.NOTE, "hilly")
+        assertEquals(
+            CardioLogRequest(
+                durationSeconds = 1992,
+                distance = CardioDistance(5.02, km),
+                avgHeartRate = 148,
+                maxHeartRate = 171,
+                calories = 410,
+                effort = 6,
+                elevationGain = 42,
+                avgCadence = 172,
+            ),
+            d.toRequest(),
+        )
+    }
+}
